{
  "text": "Principles:\n1. Develop knights and bishops early, castle by move 10.\n2. Control the center with pawns (e4/d4 or ...e5/...d5).\n3. Before each move, check: is any piece hanging? Can opponent capture or fork?\n4. Trade when ahead in material; avoid trades when behind.\n5. Do not move the same piece twice in the opening without a reason.\n6. Keep the king safe; don't open lines in front of it.\n7. In endgames: activate the king, push passed pawns.\n8. Only resign if clearly lost; otherwise look for stalemate/swindles.\n9. Take notes after each game on what the opponent does."
}
```

> 4. Trade when ahead in material; avoid trades when behind.
> 5. Do not move the same piece twice in the opening without a reason.
> 6. Keep the king safe; don't open lines in front of it.
